Sandbox_CrisisAction_DailyBoxHelp_tooltip::en = "Customize the contents and weights of the Daily Reward Box.<br>If set as an item pool, all items within the pool have an equal chance of being drawn.<br><RGB:1,1,0>Format <RGB:1,1,1>: Item;Weight;Amount <br><br><RGB:0,1,1>Item <RGB:1,1,1>: The 'CrisisAction.' prefix can be omitted for items from this mod; vanilla and other mod items require the full ID (e.g., Base.223Box).<br>Category tags supported: e.g., @partE / @partD (by part level), @ammo (ammo and magazines); or use A|B|C for custom item pools (equal chance).<br><RGB:0,1,1>Weight <RGB:1,1,1>: Relative probability.
